def lse_solver(a_init, n, L, time_limit, rng, beta_min=50, beta_max=20000, ...):
    a=normalize(a); y=a.copy(); t=1; step=...
    while time:
       c,M=conv(y)
       update best
       # weights
       if use_top: Ltop=max(16, int(beta?)) choose top lags
       else: beta ramp, w=softmax(beta*(c-M))
       A=fft(y,L); W=fft(w,L); grad=2*real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]
       # center? Project simplex function handles sum.
       # choose alpha based on step and line search maybe
       x = project_simplex(y - alpha*grad, 1)
       # update with FISTA; if M(x) > M(y) large restart t.

Detailed local optimizer `polish`:
Input a sum1, time budget. We can work with active weights schedule.
Pseudo:
```
def polish(a, n, L, time_limit, rng):
  a=normalize; c,M=conv; best=M; best_a=a
  t_acc=1; alpha=0.2
  for it in range...
    c,M=conv(a) maybe use y? Use a directly (no momentum) for line search.
    # weights: top K uniform with K=min(8+it/100, 128); or softmax
    w=weights_from_c(c)
    h=half_grad(a,w)
    g=2h
    u=g-g.mean()
    sc=max(abs(u).max(), eps); d=u/sc
    # candidate alphas: alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, maybe random 1.5
    for alpha_try in [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*1.5?]:
       step=alpha_try*alpha_bound? Actually d normalized, alpha_try dimensionless.
       check positivity bound; if invalid reduce.
       x = a - step*d; clip if slightly neg; renorm.
       Mx=conv(x).max()
       if Mx<best: best, accept x; alpha*=1.05; break
    if no accept: alpha*=0.8; if it%100==0 add noise
    # if stuck, use top K broader/narrower, or spectral/dual
```
But this computes conv for multiple candidates per iter (including a current?) We can compute current conv once; candidates conv requires forward/inverse each. If 3 candidates, 3 extra. Maybe iterate high cost but fewer needed. We can use acceptance rule based on surrogate? For polish, use line search every iter maybe 2 candidates. n=8192, time 2400, if 50k iterations with 3 conv=150k conv each 2 FFT? ~300k FFTs maybe too high. But maybe conv 8192 ~0.002s ->600s. Could be okay.

We can optimize candidate evaluation: Since direction d fixed and current c, we can precompute R and Q for d to evaluate max without FFT:
For a_new=a - σ d (with sum zero and no clipping), c(σ)=c -2σ R + σ^2 Q where R = conv(d, a) (linear), Q=conv(d,d). We need to compute R and Q. R requires fft(d) and A; Q requires ifft(D*D). We already computed h but not R. We can compute D=fft(d); R=real(ifft(A*D)); Q=real(ifft(D*D)). That's 2 extra transforms plus current c (A already). If we have A from current, R and Q add one inverse for R? A forward already, D forward, ifft(D) maybe? We need ifft(A*D) and ifft(D*D). So 2 inverse +1 forward. Current c for a needs A forward and inverse. Total 2 forward +3 inverse per line search. If we instead construct several σ and evaluate by vector ops (2n length) in numpy, no extra FFT. For 3 σ, cost small. This is much cheaper! We can do exact line search using R,Q, as long as no clipping/renormalization. If d centered and α small enough to keep a-σd positive, then valid. If some negative, we can skip or fallback. Great.

So local optimizer per iter:
- Compute A=fft(a), c=real(ifft(A*A)), M.
- Choose w (dense). Compute W=fft(w), h=real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]; g=2h; u=g-mean; d=u/sc.
- Compute D=fft(d); R=real(ifft(A*D))[:2n-1]; Q=real(ifft(D*D))[:2n-1]. (D forward extra; current A already. W forward extra.) Total high. But no candidate FFTs.
- Determine positivity polynomial? Need ensure a - σ d ≥0 for candidate σ. Compute σ_max = min(a/d_pos). We'll evaluate σ in (0, min(σ_test, σ_max*0.999)). d scaled unit max; σ maybe small.
- Evaluate max function f(σ)=max_k c_k - σ R_k + σ^2 Q_k? Because c(a-σd)=c -2σ conv(a,d)+σ^2 d*d. R=conv(a,d); c_new = c -2σ R +σ^2 Q. Correct.
- Need minimize max over k. We can find good σ by sampling, maybe golden over top active lags. Since full length 16k, sampling 20 values with vector max is maybe 20*16k=320k ops, okay in numpy. But per iter maybe heavy but okay if Python numpy C loops. We can sample log-linear σ between 0 and σ_range. Or golden using full vector (30*16k) too much. Use coarse sampling 16 points to pick best, then local refinement 10 points around.
- Since f may be nonconvex, sampling robust. Choose σ candidates: 0.1,0.2,... up to σ_max*0.95; include smaller. Scale base via α history.
- After selecting σ, compute x=a-σd; clip tiny negatives, normalize (sum should 1 if d sum zero, but numerical; renorm). It may not have sum exactly due floating but okay. Evaluate actual M via formula from R/Q at σ (or conv for exact? We can trust, but renorm changes; maybe do conv if best improvement and not too often). Update best.
- Adapt base: if accepted, increase; else reduce or change weights.

This method is gradient descent with exact line search on quadratic approximation (which is exact for c if no clipping). That's powerful and different from previous FISTA.

`h_i = ∑_{k active} a_{k-i}` for i such that 0≤k-i<n. For each k, this is a reverse segment of a of length min(n, k+1) added to h[:len]. For K=128, 128 Python loops maybe 0.0005? 1000 iters ->0.5s? Actually slice addition in Python loop overhead 128*1us=0.128ms plus memory 1M ~0.1ms, total ~0.2ms. For unit k: if k-i in [0,n-1] => i in [max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)]. h[i] += a[k-i]. So slice h[lo:hi+1] += a[k-hi : k-lo:-1]? Need reversed. Easier precompute `a_rev = a[::-1]`? For k fixed, sequence over i lo..hi: a[k-i]. In numpy: `a[k - hi : k - lo : -1]`? Example k=2,n=3: lo=0,hi=2, values [a2,a1,a0] = a[2::-1] length3. For k=1: lo=0,hi=1, [a1,a0]=a[1::-1]. def lse_opt(a,n,L,budget,rng,use_acc=True):
  a=normalize; best=convmax; ...
  sigma_frac=0.5
  alpha=0.3
  no_improve=0
  for:
    c,M=convolve_self(y,L); if M<best
    beta/sigma based on progress: sigma_frac = 0.5*0.2^progress? (from broad to narrow)
    w=weights_top_or_lse(c, K=...)
    h=half_grad(y,w,L)
    g=2h; u=g-g.mean(); d=u/max(abs)
    # alpha bound
    pos=d>1e-15; if any amax=min(a[pos]/d[pos]) else inf
    step = min(alpha, 0.98*amax)
    x=y-step*d; if x.min()< -1e-10: x=project_simplex(x,1)
    x[x<0]=0; x/=x.sum()
    # optional accept/restart: compute Mx? If periodic, else assume
    if use_acc:
       t_new=...
       y_new=x+...; if Mx > M*(1+1e-8) maybe t=1,y=a
    else: y=x
    adapt alpha
```

Implement `line_search_polish(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, use_top=True, K_start=16, K_end=512, verbose=True)`. It returns best_a, best_M, best_score.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def line_search_polish(...):
    a=normalize_sum1(a_init,n)
    A, c, M = get via fft
    best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
    base = 0.2*1.0/n  # step length
    K = K_start
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time < limit:
       # ensure A,c current? We have from previous a; at start compute.
       if M < best_M - 1e-15: best...
       # maybe periodic exact conv (already exact)
       # choose K based on progress and no_improve
       progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/time_limit)
       K_cur = int(min(K_end, max(K_start, K_start + (K_end-K_start)*progress)))
       if no_improve > 50: K_cur=min(size, max(32, K_cur*2))
       # weights
       if it % 3 == 0: # top
          w=top_weights(c, K_cur)
       else:
          w=lse_weights(c, 0.1 + 0.9*progress?) # maybe sigma_frac 0.1..0.3
       h=half_grad(a,w,A,L)
       g=2.0*h
       u=g - g.mean()
       maxu=np.max(np.abs(u))
       if maxu < 1e-300: break
       d = u / maxu  # direction to subtract
       # D, R, Q
       D=fft(d,L); R=real(ifft(A*D)[:size]); Q=real(ifft(D*D)[:size])
       # Determine positivity hi
       pos = d > 1e-12
       if np.any(pos):
           ratios = a[pos]/d[pos]
           hi = 0.95*np.min(ratios)
       else:
           hi = 10.0/n
       if hi <= 0: # cannot move positive direction, maybe use projection? 
           # try smaller or random
           hi = 1e-12
       # Search domain: We want possibly step near hi? base should be within.
       # If base > hi, candidates around hi.
       hi = min(hi, max(base*8.0, 5.0/n))? But if positivity hi small, don't exceed.
       # Candidate generation:
       hi_search = min(hi, max(hi, base*?)) no.
       cands=[]
       for factor in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0): s=hi_search*factor? But if optimum small, include base.
       full candidates: base*1, base/2, base/4, base*2, base*4, hi, hi/2, hi/4, hi*0.1
       filter 0< s < hi_search
       Also if hi_search < base, use hi_search*factors.
       # evaluate f for each s:
       # Use precompute maybe evaluate with vector:
       best_s=0; best_f=M
       for s in cands:
           f = c - 2.0*s*R + (s*s)*Q
           fm = f.max()
           if fm < best_f: best_f=fm; best_s=s
       # Refine around best_s: lo=max(0,best_s*0.6), hi= min(hi_search, best_s*1.4 or base*?)
       if best_s>0:
           lo=max(0,best_s*0.5); hi2=min(hi_search, max(best_s*1.5, lo+1e-12))
           for s in linspace(lo,hi2,12): eval
       else: # try small hi 0.1?
       if best_f < M - 1e-14:
           step=best_s
           x=a-step*d
           # due to renorm? If any negative (numer), clip
           if x.min() < 0: x=np.clip(x,0,None)
           ssum=x.sum()
           if ssum < 1e-300: x=uniform; break
           x/=ssum
           # optional exact verify if best_f < best_M or every 20
           if best_f < best_M or it%20==0:
               c_new,M_new=convolve_max(x,L,size)
               if M_new < best_M: best_M=M_new; best_a=x.copy()
               # use actual c_new as current? If verify, update c=M? For next iter, recompute A,c for x.
               A=fft(x,L); c=c_new; M=M_new
           else:
               # Use formula c_new? We need update c for next iter; can set c = c - 2*step*R + step^2*Q (if no renorm) and A? For next gradient need A of x; we can compute A=fft(x,L) but c can be formula (no inverse) to save? But next c needed; we can compute c by formula and A by forward only. That's one forward. Good.
               A=fft(x,L); c = c - 2.0*step*R + step*step*Q; M = float(c.max())
           base *= 1.05 (cap 10/n)
           no_improve=0
       else:
           # reject, maybe try random direction if many fails
           base *= 0.75
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 50:
               # perturb weights? maybe replace a with best_a plus noise?
               # Or add small random direction
               d = rng.normal(n); d-=mean; normalize; ... but too complex; simpler add noise to a and recompute
               noise=0.01*(1/n)*rng.random(n); a=normalize(a+noise*n?); recompute
               no_improve=0
       it +=1
```

Now `spectral_square` function:
```
def spectral_square(a_init,n,L,time_limit,rng):
   a=normalize; best=convmax; start=...
   # targetM maybe best_M*0.99? We'll flatten relative.
   it=0
   while time:
      A=fft(a,L); c=real(ifft(A*A))[:size]; M=...
      update
      # Modify c: we want lower peaks. Use soft power
      c_pos=np.maximum(c, 1e-30)
      # flatten: c_t = c_pos**alpha / sum, with alpha increasing from 0.6 to 0.9? If alpha<1 raises lows, lowers highs? c<1 (since sum1), c^alpha >c. So alpha<1 increases lows more, reduces relative peaks. Then normalize to sum1; max may increase if multiplied by 1/sum(c^alpha). But sum(c^alpha)>sum(c)=1 for alpha<1, so normalized max = max(c^alpha)/sum(c^alpha) = M^{alpha}/sum(c^alpha), which may be lower if M<1 and many terms? likely.
      alpha = 0.5 + 0.4*progress
      qt = np.power(c_pos, alpha)
      qt /= qt.sum()
      # Further enforce target by clipping? If qt.max() > M_target maybe not.
      Q=fft(qt,L)
      P=np.sqrt(Q)
      # enforce Hermitian:
      P = 0.5*(P + np.conjugate(P[::-1]))  # P[0] real? sqrt of real positive at 0 real.
      # P[0] should be real; maybe set real.
      a_raw = np.real(ifft(P))[:n]
      # project nonneg: a_raw = np.maximum(a_raw,0); normalize
      # blend with current to avoid jump: a_new = 0.5*a + 0.5*a_proj? Could try.
      a_new = project? If a_raw sum small? fallback
      # Maybe clip and renorm.
      # Accept if improves else try smaller: alpha? or skip.
```

def fista_opt(...):
  x=y=a; t=1; alpha=0.1; no_imp=0
  while time:
    A=fft(y,L); c=real(ifft(A*A)); M=c.max()

    pos=d>0; amax=min(y[pos]/d[pos]) if any else 10/n
    step=min(alpha,0.98*amax)
    x_new=y-step*d
    if x_new.min()< -1e-12: project
    else: x_new -= (x_new.sum()-1)/n? or normalize
    # FISTA:
    t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
    y_new = x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new - y)?? Standard: y_{k+1}=x_{k+1}+ (t_k-1)/(t_{k+1}) (x_{k+1}-x_k). Here x_k previous. Use x_prev.
    # compute M_x maybe every 5; if worse than best_y restart.
```

Function `get_fft_size(n)`:
```
from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
return next_fast_len(2*n-1)
```
But if scipy not? Allowed. We can fallback to pow2.

Function `eval_internal_score(a,n,L,size)`:
```
c,M=convolve_max(a,L,size); return 2*n*M
```

Function `convolve_max(a,L,size)`:
```
A=fft(a.real? a,L) # a real
c=ifft(A*A).real
c=c[:size]
# set tiny negative to 0? If many negative from round, not affect max maybe.
if c.max() < 0? no.
return c, float(np.max(c))
```
Clamping c to nonnegative after recompute: `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`? For exact convolution of nonnegative, negative only rounding; clamping helps. But if sequence has tiny negative internal, not. Do `c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)` maybe costs. In loops with formula, clipping could break quadratic relation; do only on recompute. For formula, can leave small negative.

Function `top_weights_and_idx(c,K)`:
```
size=len(c); K=int(min(max(1,K),size));
idx=np.argpartition(c, size-K)[-K:] if K<size else np.arange(size)
# sort? not needed
w=np.zeros(size); w[idx]=1.0/K
return w, idx
```
For K=size, w=ones/size; idx none maybe.

`lse_weights(c, sigma_frac)`:
```
m=c.max(); mn=c.min(); range=m-mn
sigma=max(1e-15, range*sigma_frac + 1e-15)
# To avoid overflow, c-m <=0
e=np.exp((c-m)/sigma)
s=e.sum()
if not finite: top
return e/s
```

`make_direction(g, use_tanh=False)`:
```
u=g-g.mean()
# remove tiny
if use_tanh:
   scale = max(1e-30, u.std())
   d=np.tanh(u/scale)
else:
   maxu=np.max(np.abs(u))
   if maxu<1e-30: return None
   d=u/maxu
d -= d.mean()
maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
if maxd<1e-30: return None
d/=maxd
return d
```

`positivity_bound(a,d)`:
```
pos=d>1e-12
if not any: return 1.0
ratios=a[pos]/d[pos]
return 0.95*float(np.min(ratios))
```

Pseudo full:
```
def line_search_polish(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng, verbose=True, K_start=16,K_end=512):
    a=normalize_sum1(a_init,n)
    A=fft(a,L); c=M?
    best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); start=...
    base=0.2/n
    no=0; it=0; tanh=False
    c_ref=None
    while time<limit:
       # if c formula error >? recompute every 50 or if no? at loop start c should current; but if a changed and not recompute enough, maybe recompute if it%50==0.
       if it%70==0:
           A=fft(a,L); c,cmax=...
           M=cmax
       if M<best_M: best...
       progress=...
       K=...
       if no>50: K=min(size, K*2)
       # choose weights
       if it % 4 == 0:
          w, active_idx = top_weights(c,K)
       else:
          w=lse_weights(c, 0.05+0.2*progress)
          # active_idx top max(K, 256) for verification subset
          active_idx = top_indices_only(c, max(K,128))
       h=half_grad(a,w,A,L)
       g=2h
       d=make_direction(g, tanh)
       if d is None: break
       D=fft(d,L); R=real(ifft(A*D))[:size]; Q=real(ifft(D*D))[:size]
       hi=positivity_bound(a,d)
       # cap step to not exceed maybe 5/n to avoid huge
       hi=min(hi, max(base*4.0, 5.0/n, 1e-12))
       if hi <= 0:
           base*=0.5; no+=1; continue
       # active arrays
       c_a = c[active_idx]; R_a=R[active_idx]; Q_a=Q[active_idx]
       factors = (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.15,0.05, base/hi if hi>0 else 0.1, base*2/hi if hi>0 else 0.1)
       cand=[]
       for f in factors: s=hi*f; if 0<s<hi: cand.append(s)
       # ensure at least hi and hi/2
       cand.append(hi*0.99); if base<hi: cand.append(base); append(base/2); append(base*2)
       unique filter.
       best_s=0; best_f=M
       for s in cand:
           f=(c_a - 2*s*R_a + s*s*Q_a).max()
           if f<best_f: ...
       if best_s>0: refine around best_s 11 steps active.
       # full verify with halving
       full_s=best_s
       while full_s>0:
           full_max=(c - 2*full_s*R + full_s*full_s*Q).max()
           if full_max < M - 1e-15: break
           full_s*=0.5
       if full_s>0 and full_max < M - 1e-15:
           # accept
           x=a-full_s*d
           x -= (x.sum()-1)/n  # tiny? Could break if negative; do safe
           if x.min() < -1e-14: x=np.maximum(x,0); x/=x.sum()
           else:
               xs=x.sum(); if xs>0: x=x/xs? This changes M slightly. To keep formula, maybe not; but sum exact? d sum ~0 due d-=mean, xsum ~1. Do not renorm if |xsum-1|<1e-8; else adjust by subtract mean (if min after adjust >0) else normalize.
           c = (c - 2*full_s*R + full_s*full_s*Q)  # full array
           # clamp tiny if not recompute? c = np.maximum(c,0) every? Could do if x normalized changed.
           A=fft(x,L)
           M=float(np.max(c))
           if M<best_M - 1e-15:
               best_M=M; best_a=x.copy()
           base*=1.1; if base>5/n: base=5/n
           no=0; tanh=False
       else:
           no+=1; base*=0.75
           if no>30: tanh=not tanh? or True
           if no>100:
               # random perturb best? 
               a=best_a.copy(); noise=0.02*(1/n)*rng.random(n); a=normalize(a+noise); recompute; no=0
       it+=1
    # final recompute best
    return best_a,best_M,2*n*best_M
```

def sanitize_direction(a,d):
   # After centering and scaling, zero out some positive d that would overly limit step.
   for _ in range(2):
      pos=d>1e-12
      if not any: break
      ratios = a[pos]/d[pos]
      med_ratio = np.median(ratios) if len>0 else 1.0
      # if smallest ratio < 0.01*med, zero them
      bad = pos.copy(); bad_ratio = a[pos] <= max(1e-12, 0.01*med_ratio*d[pos])
      d[bad_ratio]=0
      d -= d.mean()
      maxd=np.max(np.abs(d)); if maxd>0: d/=maxd
   return d
```

Let's design `spectral_square` robust and time-limited:
```
def spectral_square(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng):
  a=normalize; best=convmax; start=...
  alpha=0.55; blend=0.5; it=0
  while time<limit:
     A=fft(a); c=...
     if M<best...
     # modify c using alpha and maybe target
     c_pos=np.maximum(c,1e-30)
     # soft flatten: log domain to avoid underflow: logc=log(c); qt=exp(alpha*(logc - mean_log?))? But sum matters.
     # simpler q = c_pos ** alpha; q/=sum
     q = np.power(c_pos, alpha)
     qs=q.sum()
     if qs<...: break
     q/=qs
     Q=fft(q,L)
     P=np.sqrt(Q)
     # Hermitian:
     P=(P + np.conjugate(P[::-1]))*0.5
     # Make P[0] real (sum): P[0]=np.real(P[0])
     araw=np.real(ifft(P))[:n]
     araw=np.maximum(araw,0)
     s=araw.sum()
     if s > 0: araw/=s
     else: araw=uniform
     # blend with current to avoid wild
     newa=blend*araw + (1-blend)*a
     newa=np.maximum(newa,0); newa/=sum
     # maybe also try q clipped
     cnew,Mnew=conv(newa)
     if Mnew<best: accept, maybe increase blend direction
     else:
        # try smaller blend
        newa2=0.25*araw+0.75*a; normalize; eval; if improve accept
        else maybe alpha adjust
     # schedule alpha increase, blend random
```
`np.power(c,0.5)` for c~1e-4 okay. q sum maybe large; normalization.

Will spectral step be too disruptive? Accept only if improves, so safe. It may find improvements.

     # evaluate

Detailed top-level functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def get_fft_size(n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def normalize_sum1(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        n = x.size if x.size>0 else 256
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    x = x / s
    # add tiny floor to avoid exact zeros
    n=x.size
    floor = 1e-12
    if n>0:
        # Maintain sum 1: x = x*(1-floor) + floor/n
        x = x * (1.0 - floor) + floor / n
        x = x / x.sum()
    return x
```
But floor 1e-12/n may be below machine relative? For n=8192, 1.2e-16, could underflow? It's okay but not much effect. Use floor=1e-10? min 1e-14. Let's use `floor_frac = 1e-10` gives min 1e-14. Good.

```
def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    x=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(x)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
    t=(cum-S)/idx
    valid=t <= x
    if not valid.any(): rho=n
    else: rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1)
    theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
We mostly avoid projection.

```
def convolve_max(a, L, size):
    A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.ifft(A * A).real
    c = c[:size]
    # clamp tiny negatives
    # np.maximum(c,0,out=c) but if all negative? not
    if np.any(c < 0):
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, float(c.max())
```
`np.any(c<0)` each iter O(size), okay. Could just `c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0)` for safety.

`half_grad(a,w,A,L,n)`:
```
W=np.fft.fft(w,L)
h=np.fft.ifft(W*np.conjugate(A)).real[:n]
return h
```
Should we divide by anything? FFT no normalization; c=ifft(A*A) gives correct. DERIVATIVE? For discrete polynomial coefficients, c_k=∑ a_i a_{k-i}; derivative formula no scaling. FFT ifft includes 1/L, product A*conjA same as direct correlation no 1? Yes if A=fft (unscaled), ifft divides by L, yields circular convolution sum. Correct.

`top_weights(c, K)`:
```
size=len(c)
K=int(K)
if K>=size:
   return np.ones(size)/size, np.arange(size)
if K<=1:
   idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w, np.array([idx])
kth=size-K
idx=np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:]
w=np.zeros(size); w[idx]=1.0/K
return w, idx
```
`argpartition` may choose top K unsorted. If c has NaN? no.

`lse_weights(c, sigma_frac)`:
```
m=float(c.max()); mn=float(c.min())
sigma = max(1e-300, (m-mn)*sigma_frac)
# If sigma too small relative, fallback top? exp stable because c-m<=0
e=np.exp((c-m)/sigma)
s=e.sum()
if s < 1e-300 or not finite:
   idx=argmax; w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w
return e/s
```

`make_direction(g, use_tanh)`:
```
u=g-g.mean()
if use_tanh:
   scale = np.std(u)
   if scale < 1e-300: return None
   d=np.tanh(u/scale)
else:
   maxu=np.max(np.abs(u))
   if maxu < 1e-300: return None
   d=u/maxu
d -= d.mean()
maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
if maxd < 1e-300: return None
d/=maxd
return d
```

`prepare_bound(a,d,base)`:
```
def direction_bound(a,d,base):
    # zero out positive d with very small ratios if needed
    for _ in range(2):
        pos = d > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            ratios = a[pos] / d[pos]
            hi = 0.95 * float(np.min(ratios))
            # If hi very small, remove coords causing it
            if hi < max(1e-12, 0.2*base):
                med = float(np.median(ratios)) if ratios.size else 0.0
                thresh = max(1e-12, 0.25*med)
                bad = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=bool)
                ranks_pos = pos
                # compute limit for each positive d: a/d
                lim = np.zeros_like(d)
                np.divide(a, d, out=lim, where=pos)
                bad[pos] = lim[pos] < thresh
                if np.any(bad):
                    d[bad]=0.0
                    d -= d.mean()
                    maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
                    if maxd<1e-300: return d, 0.0
                    d/=maxd
                    continue
        pos = d > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            lim = np.zeros_like(d); np.divide(a,d,out=lim,where=pos)
            hi=0.95*float(np.min(lim[pos]))
        else:
            hi = base*10.0
        # cap hi to not too large: max(base*8, 10/n?) We don't have n here; pass max_step maybe
        return d, hi
```
Need pass `n` or `max_step`. Simpler: compute hi and cap outside.
We'll implement inline in line_search instead of separate to avoid closures.

`line_search_polish` implementation details. Let's write with no nested functions.

```
def line_search_polish(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, K_start=16, K_end=512, verbose=True):
    a = normalize_sum1(a_init)
    if a.size != n: ...
    A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    c, M = convolve_max(a,L,size)  # note convolve_max recomputes A; could reuse but okay
    best_M = M
    best_a = a.copy()
    start = time.monotonic()
    base = 0.25 / n
    min_base = 1e-6 / n
    max_base = 5.0 / n
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    tanh_mode = False
    # factor arrays maybe top-level? define inside as literals no lambda.
    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
       # ensure c current (if it%100 recompute)
       if it % 100 == 0:
           A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
           c, M = convolve_max_from_A(A,L,size)  # need function to avoid recomputing fft
           # c already clamped
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
       elapsed = time.monotonic()-start
       progress = 0.0 if time_limit<=0 else min(1.0, elapsed/time_limit)
       K = int(K_start + (K_end-K_start)*progress)
       if no_improve > 40:
           K = min(size, max(K*2, 32))
       if K <= 0: K=1
       # choose weights
       use_top = (it % 3 != 0) or (no_improve < 20)
       if use_top:
           w, active_idx = top_weights(c, K)
       else:
           w = lse_weights(c, 0.05 + 0.25*progress)
           # active_idx: top max(K,128)
           if K >= size:
              active_idx = np.arange(size)
           else:
              active_idx = np.argpartition(c, size-max(K,128))[size-max(K,128):]
       h = half_grad(a,w,A,L,n)
       g = 2.0*h
       d = make_direction(g, tanh_mode)
       if d is None:
           break
       # zero bottlenecks
       pos = d > 1e-12
       if np.any(pos):
           lim = np.zeros(n)
           np.divide(a, d, out=lim, where=pos)
           hi = 0.95 * float(np.min(lim[pos]))
           base_floor = max(1e-8/n, 1e-12)
           if hi < 0.25*base:
               med = float(np.median(lim[pos])) if pos.any() else 0.0
               thresh = max(base_floor, 0.25*med)
               bad = pos & (lim < thresh)
               if np.any(bad):
                   d = d.copy()
                   d[bad]=0.0
                   d -= d.mean()
                   maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
                   if maxd < 1e-300:
                       hi=0.0
                   else:
                       d = d/maxd
                       pos = d > 1e-12
                       if np.any(pos):
                           lim2 = np.zeros(n); np.divide(a,d,out=lim2,where=pos)
                           hi = 0.95*float(np.min(lim2[pos]))
                        else: hi=base*4
       else:
           hi = base*4
       if hi <= 0 or not np.isfinite(hi) or hi < 1e-14:
           base *= 0.7
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 50:
               a = best_a.copy()
               A = np.fft.fft(a,L); c,M=convolve_max...
               no_improve=0
           it +=1
           continue
       hi = min(hi, max_base)
       # compute R,Q
       D = np.fft.fft(d, L)
       R = np.fft.ifft(A * D).real[:size]
       Q = np.fft.ifft(D * D).real[:size]
       # active arrays
       c_a = c[active_idx]
       R_a = R[active_idx]
       Q_a = Q[active_idx]
       # candidate steps
       hi_search = hi
       best_s = 0.0
       best_f = M
       # add factors of hi and base
       # Using Python list no lambda
       cand = []
       for f in (0.999, 0.7, 0.45, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05):
           s = hi_search * f
           if s > 0 and s < hi_search:
               cand.append(s)
       if base < hi_search:
           cand.append(base)
           cand.append(0.5*base)
           cand.append(2.0*base)
       else:
           cand.append(0.5*hi_search)
           cand.append(0.25*hi_search)
       # evaluate unique approximate
       seen=[]
       for s in cand:
           if s <= 0 or s >= hi_search - 1e-15: # allow 0.999
              s2 = min(s, 0.999*hi_search)
           else: s2=s
           # skip duplicates
           dup=False
           for ts in seen:
              if abs(ts-s2)<=1e-15:
                 dup=True; break
           if dup: continue
           fval = float(np.max(c_a - 2.0*s2*R_a + (s2*s2)*Q_a))
           if fval < best_f:
              best_f=fval; best_s=s2
           seen.append(s2)
       # refine if best_s
       if best_s > 0:
          lo = max(0.0, best_s*0.6)
          hi2 = min(hi_search, max(best_s*1.4, lo + 1e-12))
          # 11 points
          for j in range(11):
             s2 = lo + (hi2-lo)*j/10.0
             if s2<=0 or s2>=hi_search: continue
             fval = float(np.max(c_a - 2.0*s2*R_a + (s2*s2)*Q_a))
             if fval < best_f:
                 best_f=fval; best_s=s2
       # full verify and halve
       full_s=best_s
       full_max=M
       if full_s > 0:
          for _ in range(10):
             full_max = float(np.max(c - 2.0*full_s*R + (full_s*full_s)*Q))
             if full_max < M - 1e-15:
                 break
             full_s *= 0.5
       if full_s > 0 and full_max < M - 1e-15:
          x = a - full_s*d
          # handle negativity/sum
          changed = False
          if x.min() < -1e-12:
              x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
              xs = x.sum()
              if xs > 0:
                  x = x / xs
              else:
                  x = np.full(n,1.0/n)
              changed = True
          else:
              xs = x.sum()
              if abs(xs - 1.0) > 1e-9:
                  x = x / xs
                  changed = True
              elif abs(xs - 1.0) > 1e-13:
                  # shift by constant, check min
                  x = x + (1.0 - xs) / n
                  if x.min() < -1e-12:
                      x = np.maximum(x,0); x/=x.sum(); changed=True
       if changed:
           A = np.fft.fft(x, L)
           c, M = convolve_max_from_A(A,L,size)
       else:
           A = np.fft.fft(x, L)
           c = c - 2.0*full_s*R + (full_s*full_s)*Q
           # clamp only if needed? If c has < -1e-12, clamp? It should not far.
           if np.any(c < -1e-12):
               c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
           M = float(np.max(c))
           c = np.maximum(c,0.0)  # maybe expensive but okay? Actually np.any above plus max; do once.
       a = x
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M = M; best_a = a.copy()
       base *= 1.08
       if base > max_base: base = max_base
       no_improve = 0
       if not tanh_mode and it > 500:
           tanh_mode = (it % 1000) < 200 # occasionally
       if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
           print("  line_search it=%d M=%.8f score=%.6f base=%.3e no=%d" % (it, M, 2*n*M, base, no_improve))
       it += 1
       # if time left tiny break
    # final recompute best
    _, best_M = convolve_max(best_a, L, size)
    return best_a, best_M, 2.0*n*best_M
```

def convolve_max_from_A(A,L,size):
    c=np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:size]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c, float(c.max())

def fista_fast(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng,verbose):
   x=normalize; y=x.copy(); t=1; base=0.2/n; best=convmax; no=0; start=...
   while time:
      A=np.fft.fft(y,L); c,M=conv_from_A
      if M<best: best
      progress=...
      K=...
      if it%3: w,act=top_weights(c,K) else w=lse_weights(c,0.1)
      h=half_grad(y,w,A,L,n); d=make_direction(2*h, False)
      if d is None break
      pos=d>1e-12
      if any: hi=0.95*min(y[pos]/d[pos]) else hi=base*4
      step=min(base, hi)
      x_new = y - step*d
      if x_new.min()<0: x_new=project_simplex(x_new,1)
      else:
         xs=x_new.sum(); if not close: x_new/=xs
      # acceleration
      t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
      y_new = x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new - x)
      # Keep y in simplex? Let's project:
      if y_new.min()<0: y_new=project_simplex(y_new,1)
      else:
         ys=y_new.sum(); if not close: y_new/=ys
      x=x_new; y=y_new; t=t_new
      # periodic actual eval of x_new and restart
      if it%20==0:
         _, Mx=conv_max_only(x_new,L,size)
         if Mx < best - 1e-15:
             best=Mx; best_a=x_new.copy()
         if Mx > M + 1e-12 or it-no>100:
             # no improvement, maybe restart or reduce base
             base *=0.8
             if no>50:
                t=1; y=x.copy()
         else:
             base *=1.05
      no +=1; it+=1

Hermitian correction: `P[::-1]` maps index 0 to last (L-1), which corresponds frequency -0? For DFT length L, Hermitian symmetry: P[k] = conj(P[(-k) mod L]). Need define `resize_sequence(a, new_n)` top-level:
```
def resize_sequence(a,new_n):
   old_n=a.size
   if old_n==new_n: return a.copy()
   if new_n>old_n:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
       return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   else:
       nb=old_n//new_n
       if nb<=0: return np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,new_n),np.linspace(0,1,old_n),a)
       return a[:nb*new_n].reshape(new_n,nb).mean(axis=1)
```

def fista_fast(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng,verbose=True):
   a=normalize_sum1(a_init)
   x=a.copy(); y=a.copy(); t=1.0
   A=fft(x,L); c,M=conv_from_A(A,size)
   best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
   base=0.15/n; min_base=1e-6/n; max_base=5/n
   no=0; start=time.monotonic(); it=0
   while time<limit:
      # Use y for eval? We have c maybe of x? Let's recompute y each iter.
      A=np.fft.fft(y,L); c,M=conv_from_A(A,size)
      if M<best_M: best_M=M; best_a=x.copy()? Actually best of y? y may be extrapolated outside simplex? We project y so objective valid. If y projected, okay. But we track x too. If M<best, best_a=y.copy()
      progress=...
      K=int(8+...); if no>30 double
      if it%2: top else lse
      h=half_grad(y,w,A,L,n); d=make_direction(2*h, False)
      if d None break
      # bound
      pos=d>1e-12; hi=...
      step=min(base,hi)
      x_new=y-step*d
      if x_new.min()<0: x_new=project_simplex(x_new,1)
      else:
         xs=x_new.sum(); if abs(xs-1)>1e-9: x_new/=xs
      # acceleration
      t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
      y_new=x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new-x)
      if y_new.min()< -1e-12: y_new=project_simplex(y_new,1)
      else:
         ys=y_new.sum(); if abs(ys-1)>1e-9: y_new/=ys
      # Periodic eval of x_new
      if it%25==0:
         _, Mx=conv_max_only(x_new,L,size)
         if Mx<best_M: best=Mx; best_a=x_new.copy(); no=0; base*=1.05
         else:
            no+=1; base*=0.85
            if no>80:
               t=1.0; y=x_new.copy(); base=0.15/n
      x=x_new; y=y_new; t=t_new; it+=1
   _, best_M=conv_max_only(best_a,L,size)
   return best_a,best_M,2*n*best_M
```

def spectral_square(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng):
   a=normalize; A,c,M=...; best...
   start=...
   alpha=0.6; blend=0.5; no=0
   while time:
      # current c maybe from a; recompute at loop start
      A=fft(a); c,M=conv_from_A(A,size); update
      progress=...
      alpha = 0.5 + 0.45*progress
      # q flatten
      cpos=np.maximum(c,1e-30)
      q=np.power(cpos, alpha)
      qs=q.sum()
      if qs<1e-300: break
      q/=qs
      Q=np.fft.fft(q,L)
      P=np.sqrt(Q)
      P=0.5*(P+np.conjugate(P[::-1]))
      P[0]=float(np.real(P[0]))
      raw=np.real(np.fft.ifft(P))[:n]
      raw=np.maximum(raw,0)
      s=raw.sum()
      if s<1e-15: raw=np.ones(n)
      else: raw/=s
      # try blends
      for b in (blend, 0.25, 0.75):
         trial=b*raw + (1-b)*a
         trial=np.maximum(trial,0)
         ts=trial.sum()
         if ts<1e-15: continue
         trial/=ts
         _, Mt=conv_max_only(trial,L,size)
         if Mt < best_M - 1e-15:
             best_M=Mt; best_a=trial.copy(); no=0
             a=trial.copy()
             break
      else:
         no+=1
         blend = 0.5*blend? or random
         if no>20: alpha = min(0.95, alpha+0.05)
   final recompute
```
This recomputes conv for up to 3 trials per iter: 3*2 transforms each, heavy. But spectral time limited. We can reduce to one trial per iter with adaptive blend. Use if no improve, change blend random.
```
blend_current = 0.5
for iter:
   trial = blend_current*raw + (1-blend_current)*a
   eval one
   if improve: adjust blend maybe increase
   else: blend_current *=0.7; if no>20 recompute best
```

def dual_multiplicative(a_init,n,L,size,time_limit,rng):
   a=normalize; best=...
   start=...
   power=0.4; sigma=0.15; no=0
   while time:
      A=fft(a); c,M=conv_from_A
      if M<best...
      w=lse_weights(c,sigma)
      W=fft(w,L)
      inner=12
      for _ in range(inner):
          # if time break
          A=fft(a,L)
          h=real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]
          hmean=float(np.mean(h))
          if hmean <=0: break
          loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
          upd=power*(np.log(hmean+1e-300)-np.log(h+1e-300))
          upd=np.clip(upd,-1.5,1.5)
          loga2=loga+upd
          loga2 -= loga2.max()
          a=np.exp(loga2)
          a=np.maximum(a,0)
          s=a.sum()
          if s<1e-15: a=np.ones(n)
          else: a/=s
          # normalize floor? no
          _,M=conv_max_only(a,L,size)
          if M<best_M:
             best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
      # outer: if no improvement overall maybe revert
      if best_a is best? no. At start of outer, store old_best. If after inner best_M not improved, revert a to best_a.
```

Top-level:
```
def conv_max_only(a,L,size):
    A=np.fft.fft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:size]
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

def conv_from_A(A,L,size):
    c=np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:size]
    if np.any(c < 0): c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c, float(np.max(c))
```

import numpy as np
import time
import math

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def get_fft_size(n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    return next_pow2(m)

def time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
...

def soft_normalize(a):
    a=np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=False).reshape(-1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

under the evaluator's non-negative clipping.

The implementation combines:
  - accelerated projected gradient with top-lag / softmax active sets,
  - exact one-dimensional quadratic line search for centered moves,
  - spectral square-root projection in the polynomial-square domain,
  - multiplicative dual-field updates,
  - diverse starts from the supplied parent plus smooth/spectral variants.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def get_fft_size(n):
    return next_pow2(2 * int(n) - 1)


def time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())


def soft_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def initialize_sequence(a):
    x = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x / s
    # Tiny floor avoids exact zeros and makes projected moves less brittle.
    floor = 1e-10
    x = x * (1.0 - floor) + floor / x.size
    return x / x.sum()


def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = tc <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def resize_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    if old_n <= 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a.sum() if a.size else 1.0), dtype=np.float64)
    if new_n > old_n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, endpoint=False)
        return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    nb = old_n // new_n
    if nb <= 0:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, endpoint=False)
        return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return a[:nb * new_n].reshape(new_n, nb).mean(axis=1)


def make_palindromic(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < n:
        a = np.resize(a, n)
    half = a[:n // 2]
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        out[:n // 2] = half
        out[n // 2:] = half[::-1]
    else:
        out[:n // 2] = half
        out[n // 2] = a[n // 2]
        out[n // 2 + 1:] = half[::-1]
    return out


def conv_max_only(a, L, size):
    A = np.fft.fft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), L)
    c = np.fft.ifft(A * A).real[:size]
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return 0.0
    return m


def conv_from_A(A, L, size):
    c = np.fft.ifft(A * A).real[:size]
    if c.size == 0:
        return c, 0.0
    if np.any(c < 0.0):
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, float(np.max(c))


def half_grad(a, w, A, L, n):
    W = np.fft.fft(w, L)
    return np.fft.ifft(W * np.conjugate(A)).real[:n]


def top_weights(c, K):
    size = len(c)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= size:
        w = np.full(size, 1.0 / size, dtype=np.float64)
        return w, np.arange(size)
    if K == 1:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w, np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64)
    kth = size - K
    idx = np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:]
    w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    return w, idx


def lse_weights(c, sigma_frac):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    mn = float(np.min(c))
    rng_v = m - mn
    sigma = max(1e-300, rng_v * float(sigma_frac))
    e = np.exp((c - m) / sigma)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(size(len(c)), dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def make_direction(g, use_tanh=False):
    u = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64) - float(np.mean(g))
    if not np.any(np.isfinite(u)):
        return None
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    if use_tanh:
        scale = float(np.std(u))
        if scale <= 1e-300:
            return None
        d = np.tanh(u / scale)
    else:
        maxu = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
        if maxu <= 1e-300:
            return None
        d = u / maxu
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxd <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return d / maxd


def fista_fast(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, verbose=True):
    a = initialize_sequence(a_init)
    if a.size != n:
        a = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(a, n))

    x = a.copy()
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    A = np.fft.fft(x, L)
    c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)

    best_M = M
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    base = 0.18 / n
    min_base = 1e-6 / n
    max_base = 6.0 / n
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        if it % 80 == 0:
            A = np.fft.fft(y, L)
            c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = y.copy()

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        progress = 0.0 if time_limit <= 0 else min(1.0, elapsed / time_limit)
        K = int(24 + 360 * progress)
        if no_improve > 40:
            K = min(size, max(64, K * 2))

        if it % 2 == 0:
            w, _ = top_weights(c, K)
        else:
            w = lse_weights(c, 0.08 + 0.22 * progress)

        h = half_grad(y, w, A, L, n)
        d = make_direction(2.0 * h, False)
        if d is None:
            break

        pos = d > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            lim = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            np.divide(y, d, out=lim, where=pos)
            hi = 0.95 * float(np.min(lim[pos]))
        else:
            hi = max_base

        step = base
        if np.isfinite(hi) and hi > 0:
            step = min(step, max(0.0, hi))
        if step <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(step):
            base = max(min_base, base * 0.7)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 80:
                t = 1.0
                y = x.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        x_new = y - step * d
        if x_new.min() < -1e-12:
            x_new = project_simplex(x_new, 1.0)
        else:
            xs = float(x_new.sum())
            if abs(xs - 1.0) > 1e-9 or not np.isfinite(xs):
                x_new = soft_normalize(x_new)

        x_old = x
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y_new = x_new + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x_new - x_old)
        if y_new.min() < -1e-12:
            y_new = project_simplex(y_new, 1.0)
        else:
            ys = float(y_new.sum())
            if abs(ys - 1.0) > 1e-9 or not np.isfinite(ys):
                y_new = soft_normalize(y_new)

        x = x_new
        y = y_new
        t = t_new

        if it % 25 == 0:
            Mx = conv_max_only(x_new, L, size)
            if Mx < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = Mx
                best_a = x_new.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                base = min(max_base, base * 1.08)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                base = max(min_base, base * 0.88)
                if no_improve > 90:
                    t = 1.0
                    y = x_new.copy()
                    base = 0.18 / n
                    no_improve = 0
                    A = np.fft.fft(y, L)
                    c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(
                "  fista_fast it=%d M=%.8f score=%.6f base=%.3e no=%d"
                % (it, M, 2.0 * n * M, base, no_improve)
            )
        it += 1

    best_a = soft_normalize(best_a)
    A = np.fft.fft(best_a, L)
    _, best_M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    return best_a, best_M, 2.0 * n * best_M


def line_search_polish(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, K_start=16, K_end=600, verbose=True):
    a = initialize_sequence(a_init)
    if a.size != n:
        a = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(a, n))

    A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    best_M = M
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    base = 0.25 / n
    min_base = 1e-6 / n
    max_base = 6.0 / n
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    tanh_mode = False

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        if it % 100 == 0:
            A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
            c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        progress = 0.0 if time_limit <= 0 else min(1.0, elapsed / time_limit)
        K = int(K_start + (K_end - K_start) * progress)
        if no_improve > 40:
            K = min(size, max(64, K * 2))
        K = max(1, K)

        if it % 3 == 0 or no_improve < 20:
            w, active_idx = top_weights(c, K)
        else:
            w = lse_weights(c, 0.05 + 0.25 * progress)
            K_lim = max(K, 128)
            if K_lim >= size:
                active_idx = np.arange(size)
            else:
                active_idx = np.argpartition(c, size - K_lim)[size - K_lim:]

        h = half_grad(a, w, A, L, n)
        d = make_direction(2.0 * h, tanh_mode)
        if d is None:
            break

        # Remove small coordinates that would make the positivity step too small.
        for _ in range(2):
            pos = d > 1e-12
            if not np.any(pos):
                break
            lim = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            np.divide(a, d, out=lim, where=pos)
            hi0 = 0.95 * float(np.min(lim[pos]))
            if hi0 >= 0.25 * base:
                break
            med = float(np.median(lim[pos])) if np.any(pos) else 0.0
            thresh = max(1e-8 / n, 0.1 * med)
            bad = pos & (lim < thresh)
            if not np.any(bad):
                break
            d = d.copy()
            d[bad] = 0.0
            d = d - float(np.mean(d))
            maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if maxd <= 1e-300:
                break
            d = d / maxd

        pos = d > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            lim = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            np.divide(a, d, out=lim, where=pos)
            hi = 0.95 * float(np.min(lim[pos]))
        else:
            hi = max_base

        if not np.isfinite(hi) or hi <= 1e-14:
            base = max(min_base, base * 0.7)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 80:
                a = best_a.copy()
                A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
                c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
                no_improve = 0
                base = 0.25 / n
            it += 1
            continue

        hi = min(hi, max_base)

        D = np.fft.fft(d, L)
        R = np.fft.ifft(A * D).real[:size]
        Q = np.fft.ifft(D * D).real[:size]

        c_a = c[active_idx]
        R_a = R[active_idx]
        Q_a = Q[active_idx]

        best_s = 0.0
        best_f = M

        candidates = []
        hi_f = (0.999, 0.75, 0.50, 0.30, 0.15, 0.075, 0.030, 0.010)
        for f in hi_f:
            s = hi * f
            if s > 0.0:
                candidates.append(s)
        if base < hi:
            candidates.extend((base, 0.5 * base, 2.0 * base, 4.0 * base))
        else:
            candidates.extend((0.5 * hi, 0.25 * hi, 0.125 * hi))

        seen = []
        for s in candidates:
            if s <= 0.0 or s >= hi:
                continue
            dup = False
            for ts in seen:
                if abs(ts - s) <= 1e-15:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if dup:
                continue
            seen.append(s)
            fval = float(np.max(c_a - 2.0 * s * R_a + (s * s) * Q_a))
            if fval < best_f:
                best_f = fval
                best_s = s

        if best_s > 0.0:
            lo = max(0.0, 0.6 * best_s)
            hi2 = min(hi, max(1.4 * best_s, lo + 1e-14))
            for j in range(11):
                s2 = lo + (hi2 - lo) * (j / 10.0)
                if s2 <= 0.0 or s2 >= hi:
                    continue
                fval = float(np.max(c_a - 2.0 * s2 * R_a + (s2 * s2) * Q_a))
                if fval < best_f:
                    best_f = fval
                    best_s = s2

        full_s = best_s
        full_c = None
        full_max = M
        while full_s > 0.0:
            full_c = c - 2.0 * full_s * R + (full_s * full_s) * Q
            full_max = float(np.max(full_c))
            if full_max < M - 1e-15:
                break
            full_s *= 0.5

        if full_s > 0.0 and full_c is not None and full_max < M - 1e-15:
            x = a - full_s * d
            changed = False
            if x.min() < -1e-12:
                x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
                x = soft_normalize(x)
                changed = True
            else:
                xs = float(x.sum())
                if abs(xs - 1.0) > 1e-9 or not np.isfinite(xs):
                    x = soft_normalize(x)
                    changed = True
                elif abs(xs - 1.0) > 1e-13:
                    x = x + (1.0 - xs) / n
                    if x.min() < -1e-12:
                        x = soft_normalize(x)
                        changed = True

            A = np.fft.fft(x, L)
            if changed:
                c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
            else:
                c = np.maximum(full_c, 0.0)
                M = float(np.max(c))

            a = x
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()

            base = min(max_base, base * 1.08)
            no_improve = 0
            if not tanh_mode and it > 700:
                tanh_mode = (it % 1000) < 150
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            base = max(min_base, base * 0.75)
            if no_improve > 30:
                tanh_mode = (not tanh_mode)
            if no_improve > 120:
                a = best_a.copy()
                noise_scale = 0.025 / n
                a = a + noise_scale * rng.random(n)
                a = soft_normalize(a)
                A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
                c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
                no_improve = 0
                tanh_mode = False
                base = 0.25 / n

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(
                "  line_search it=%d M=%.8f score=%.6f base=%.3e no=%d tanh=%d"
                % (it, M, 2.0 * n * M, base, no_improve, int(tanh_mode))
            )
        it += 1

    best_a = soft_normalize(best_a)
    A = np.fft.fft(best_a, L)
    _, best_M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    return best_a, best_M, 2.0 * n * best_M


def spectral_square(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, verbose=True):
    a = initialize_sequence(a_init)
    if a.size != n:
        a = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(a, n))

    A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    best_M = M
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    blend = 0.45
    alpha = 0.55
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
        c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        progress = 0.0 if time_limit <= 0 else min(1.0, elapsed / time_limit)
        alpha = min(0.95, 0.50 + 0.45 * progress)

        cp = np.maximum(c, 1e-30)
        q = np.power(cp, alpha)
        qs = float(q.sum())
        if qs <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(qs):
            break
        q = q / qs

        Q = np.fft.fft(q, L)
        P = np.sqrt(Q)
        P = 0.5 * (P + np.conjugate(P[::-1]))
        P[0] = float(np.real(P[0]))

        raw = np.real(np.fft.ifft(P))[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(raw)):
            raw = a.copy()
        raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
        rs = float(raw.sum())
        if rs <= 1e-15:
            raw = a.copy()
        else:
            raw = raw / rs

        trial = blend * raw + (1.0 - blend) * a
        trial = soft_normalize(trial)
        Mt = conv_max_only(trial, L, size)

        if Mt < best_M - 1e-15:
            a = trial.copy()
            best_M = Mt
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            blend = min(0.9, blend * 1.1)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            blend *= 0.75
            if no_improve > 18:
                a = best_a.copy()
                alpha = min(0.98, alpha + 0.05)
                blend = float(rng.random() * 0.6 + 0.2)
                A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
                c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
                no_improve = 0

        if verbose and it % 80 == 0:
            print(
                "  spectral_sq it=%d M=%.8f score=%.6f blend=%.2f alpha=%.2f no=%d"
                % (it, M, 2.0 * n * M, blend, alpha, no_improve)
            )
        it += 1

    best_a = soft_normalize(best_a)
    A = np.fft.fft(best_a, L)
    _, best_M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    return best_a, best_M, 2.0 * n * best_M


def dual_multiplicative(a_init, n, L, size, time_limit, rng, verbose=True):
    a = initialize_sequence(a_init)
    if a.size != n:
        a = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(a, n))

    A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
    c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    best_M = M
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    power = 0.45
    sigma_frac = 0.16
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        old_best = best_M
        A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
        c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        progress = 0.0 if time_limit <= 0 else min(1.0, elapsed / time_limit)
        sigma_frac = min(0.45, 0.08 + 0.35 * progress)
        w = lse_weights(c, sigma_frac)
        W = np.fft.fft(w, L)

        inner = 8 + int(10 * progress)
        for _ in range(inner):
            if time.monotonic() - start >= time_limit:
                break
            A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
            h = half_grad(a, w, A, L, n)
            hmean = float(np.mean(h))
            if hmean <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hmean):
                break

            loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
            upd = power * (np.log(hmean + 1e-300) - np.log(h + 1e-300))
            upd = np.clip(upd, -1.8, 1.8)
            loga2 = loga + upd
            loga2 = loga2 - float(np.max(loga2))
            a_new = np.exp(loga2)
            a = soft_normalize(a_new)

            Mt = conv_max_only(a, L, size)
            if Mt < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = Mt
                best_a = a.copy()

        if best_M < old_best - 1e-15:
            no_improve = 0
            power = min(1.2, power * 1.05)
            sigma_frac = max(0.05, sigma_frac * 0.95)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            a = best_a.copy()
            power = max(0.15, power * 0.85)
            sigma_frac = min(0.6, sigma_frac * 1.15)
            if no_improve > 25:
                A = np.fft.fft(a, L)
                c, M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
                no_improve = 0

        if verbose and it % 20 == 0:
            print(
                "  dual_mult it=%d M=%.8f score=%.6f power=%.2f sigma=%.2f no=%d"
                % (it, M, 2.0 * n * M, power, sigma_frac, no_improve)
            )
        it += 1

    best_a = soft_normalize(best_a)
    A = np.fft.fft(best_a, L)
    _, best_M = conv_from_A(A, L, size)
    return best_a, best_M, 2.0 * n * best_M


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Top-level search entry point. Returns a Python list of non-negative heights."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if isinstance(seed, (int, float)) and not isinstance(seed, bool) and seed > 1000.0:
        budget_s = float(seed)
        seed = int(kwargs.get("seed", 42))
    for key in ("time_limit", "timeout", "budget", "max_time"):
        if key in kwargs:
            budget_s = float(kwargs[key])
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, budget_s - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load optional parent construction.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)  # noqa: F821
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 128 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-6:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and 1024 <= parent.size <= 16384:
        n = int(parent.size)
    elif parent is not None and parent.size >= 512:
        n = min(int(parent.size), 8192)
        parent = resize_sequence(parent, n)
    else:
        n = 8192

    L = get_fft_size(n)
    size = 2 * n - 1

    # Build diverse starts.
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        par = initialize_sequence(parent)
        if par.size != n:
            par = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(par, n))
        starts.append(par)
        starts.append(soft_normalize(make_palindromic(par, n)))

        F = np.fft.fft(par, L)
        freqs = np.fft.fftfreq(L)
        mask = np.exp(-4.0 * (freqs / 0.25) ** 2)
        lp = np.real(np.fft.ifft(F * mask))[:n]
        hp = np.real(np.fft.ifft(F * (1.0 - mask)))[:n]
        starts.append(soft_normalize(lp))
        starts.append(soft_normalize(hp))
        starts.append(soft_normalize(par + (0.05 / n) * rng.random(n)))
        starts.append(soft_normalize(par * np.exp(-0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n))))

    uniform = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    starts.append(uniform)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    starts.append(soft_normalize(0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)))
    starts.append(soft_normalize(np.exp(-16.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 2)))
    starts.append(soft_normalize(0.2 + np.sin(np.pi * t) ** 0.75))
    starts.append(soft_normalize(np.abs(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)) ** 2 + 0.05))

    best_a = None
    best_M = np.inf
    best_score = np.inf

    for a0 in starts:
        if a0.size != n:
            a0 = soft_normalize(resize_sequence(a0, n))
        if a0.size != n:
            continue
        a0 = soft_normalize(a0)
        M = conv_max_only(a0, L, size)
        score = 2.0 * n * M
        if M < best_M:
            best_M = M
            best_score = score
            best_a = a0.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = uniform.copy()
        best_score = 2.0
        best_M = 1.0 / n

    best_n = n
    best_L = L
    best_size = size
    target_score = 1.5027

    print(
        "Start: n=%d L=%d len_parent=%s best_score=%.6f"
        % (best_n, best_L, parent.size if parent is not None else "None", best_score)
    )

    # Main accelerated gradient phase.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 80:
        t_phase = min(rem, max(60.0, rem * 0.35))
        print("\n=== Phase 1: fast FISTA-like gradient (%.0fs) ===" % t_phase)
        a1, M1, S1 = fista_fast(best_a, best_n, best_L, best_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True)
        if S1 < best_score:
            best_a = a1
            best_M = M1
            best_score = S1
            best_n = n
            best_L = L
            best_size = size
        print("After phase 1: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Main exact line-search polish.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 80:
        t_phase = max(60.0, rem * 0.42)
        print("\n=== Phase 2: active-set line search (%.0fs) ===" % t_phase)
        a2, M2, S2 = line_search_polish(best_a, best_n, best_L, best_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True)
        if S2 < best_score:
            best_a = a2
            best_M = M2
            best_score = S2
        print("After phase 2: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Spectral square-root projection phase.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 240 and best_score > target_score * 0.995:
        t_phase = min(360.0, rem * 0.14)
        print("\n=== Phase 3: spectral square rooting (%.0fs) ===" % t_phase)
        a3, M3, S3 = spectral_square(best_a, best_n, best_L, best_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True)
        if S3 < best_score:
            best_a = a3
            best_M = M3
            best_score = S3
        print("After phase 3: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Multiplicative dual-field phase.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 180 and best_score > target_score * 0.995:
        t_phase = min(260.0, rem * 0.12)
        print("\n=== Phase 4: multiplicative dual field (%.0fs) ===" % t_phase)
        a4, M4, S4 = dual_multiplicative(best_a, best_n, best_L, best_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True)
        if S4 < best_score:
            best_a = a4
            best_M = M4
            best_score = S4
        print("After phase 4: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Optional longer length.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 900 and best_n <= 8192 and best_score > target_score * 1.005:
        alt_n = 16384
        alt_L = get_fft_size(alt_n)
        alt_size = 2 * alt_n - 1
        t_phase = min(600.0, rem * 0.32)
        print(
            "\n=== Phase 5: alternate length n=%d (%.0fs) ==="
            % (alt_n, t_phase)
        )
        alt_start = resize_sequence(best_a, alt_n)
        alt_start = soft_normalize(alt_start)
        hoff, moff, seoff = line_search_polish(
            alt_start, alt_n, alt_L, alt_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True
        )
        if seoff < best_score:
            best_a = hoff
            best_M = moff
            best_score = seoff
            best_n = alt_n
            best_L = alt_L
            best_size = alt_size
            print("  alt length improved: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Final polish with remaining time.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 60:
        t_phase = max(20.0, rem * 0.85)
        print("\n=== Final polish (%.0fs) ===" % t_phase)
        af, Mf, Sf = line_search_polish(best_a, best_n, best_L, best_size, t_phase, rng, verbose=True)
        if Sf < best_score:
            best_a = af
            best_M = Mf
            best_score = Sf
        print("After final polish: %.6f" % best_score)

    # Final normalization and safety.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        s = best_a.size
    best_a = best_a / s
    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    try:
        final_score = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        final_score = np.inf

    print(
        "\nReturn: length=%d internal_score=%.9f evaluated=%.9f elapsed=%.0fs"
        % (
            len(final_list),
            best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else np.inf,
            final_score if np.isfinite(final_score) else np.inf,
            time.monotonic() - t0,
        )
    )

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        fallback = [1.0] * 512
        try:
            fs = evaluate_sequence(fallback)
            if np.isfinite(fs):
                final_list = fallback
                final_score = fs
        except Exception:
            pass

    return final_list
```
